# Evidence snapshot: r4-calibration-v1

Source commit: `f435286598ca8582164e620fa80331256a254bd0`

## Run and verification notes

R4 first fixed-corpus prefix-bound calibration, published as a new batch on 2026-09-08.

Source commit for this publication: f435286598ca8582164e620fa80331256a254bd0. Original execution used cef5b92954571423b10a0c3b56947a4b26400d3c plus the three R4 analysis files; those files remain byte-identical to results/r4_source_v1/analysis. The source ZIP is a complete git archive of the publication source commit, including dependencies and tests. Extract it into a separate directory for source inspection, or into this snapshot directory for reproduction. Original reports and run_context.json retain their execution-time state; statements that evidence was local describe the pre-publication state. This snapshot is published only after the freezing tool confirms the remote commit and complete tree.

Python 3.12.14, NumPy 2.3.5, SciPy 1.18.1; the repository .venv/Scripts/python.exe ran the checks. F4 configuration, all graph seeds, budgets, source identities and original run outputs are unchanged. The R2 input comes from immutable commit 4a419f338d70068fa988fa97027734cdcda0a036. Included source inputs retain the results/r4_source/results layout expected by the R4 usage guide.

Population: all 1,800 original R2 graphs, 13,000 budget records and 65 cells; the 18-graph/130-budget preflight is separate. There is no new sampling or enlarged experiment. Bound: U=min(B, min_t(f(P_t)+k*Delta_t)); U_initial=min(B,k*Delta_0). All records satisfy G<=O<=U<=U_initial<=B. There is one strict tightening and zero new optimality certifications; both bounds certify 10,355 records. U>G alone does not prove Greedy failure. The calibration is descriptive on this fixed corpus, not independent new-sample confirmation or a large-instance performance guarantee.

Historical commands and results are preserved in analysis/r4_usage.zh-CN.md, the original execution.jsonl, command/process logs, run_context.json and report. Original cumulative research time including preflight and report calculations was 706.6559967000027 seconds. Publication revalidation was performed on a byte-for-byte copy of the completed calibration, retaining its prior cumulative resource accounting; original experiment timings were not overwritten. The copied execution.jsonl records additional verification time under the existing 3,600-second limit. Test and Git publication overhead are separate from formal research time.

Actual publication checks:
- python scripts/check.py --profile full: 489 tests, 488 passed, one optional OR-Tools skip; mypy passed for 38 source files. See results/r4_publication_full_check.log.
- python analysis/validate_r4_prefix_bounds.py --source results/r4_source/results/r2_grid_v1 --output results/r4_publication_verification_v1/calibration: passed for all 1,800 graphs; independently rechecked seed association, paths, integer bounds, exhaustive optima and canonical witnesses.
- python analysis/validate_r4_prefix_bounds.py --output results/r4_publication_verification_v1/calibration --summaries-only: passed for all 13,000 rows and 65 cells.
- Byte comparison: current three R4 calculation files equal original execution snapshots; the original configuration, graph records, CSVs and report facts equal the revalidated copy. No production or analysis regeneration ran during publication.

The publication verification directory includes only new verification outputs/logs, not another duplicate population. To rerun the same commands, first copy results/r4_calibration_v1 into its calibration subdirectory. Independent agent review of the original implementation and report is historical and documented in F4/report notes; this publication pass reruns the independent validator and existing tests, without claiming a new external peer review. No new failures occurred in the successful scientific checks; the existing logs retain their original history. The remote evidence-snapshots ruleset was checked active for refs/heads/codex/evidence/* with deletion and non-fast-forward protection. Always cite the returned commit SHA, since the ruleset does not itself forbid fast-forward commits.
